# The design tokens of AfterNight 1.1, for every combination the interface settings can choose:
# two themes, four contrast profiles, five accent families and six surface tints.
#
# [every combination] holds what no choice changes: the spacing, the radii and the type scale,
# the fonts stated against the body font, which is the host's. Every other section is one
# combination - [theme contrast accent tint] - and holds its colours as #AARRGGBB: the colour
# roles, the surface layers and the material fallbacks, resolved with the native backdrop off.
#
# TokenBaseline.ClassicValuesAreRecorded in Test_ThemeSystem compares the running resolution
# with this record. An entry is edited by the change that means to move its value; the record is
# not written again whole.

[every combination]
spacing.xs | 4
spacing.sm | 8
spacing.md | 12
spacing.lg | 16
spacing.xl | 24
radius.none | 0
radius.sm | 4
radius.md | 8
radius.lg | 12
radius.xl | 14
typography.heading1 | the body's size plus 4 | weight 700 | the body's family | the body's style hint
typography.heading2 | the body's size plus 2 | weight 600 | the body's family | the body's style hint
typography.heading3 | the body's size plus 1 | weight 600 | the body's family | the body's style hint
typography.caption | the body's size less 1, and 8 at least | the body's weight | the body's family | the body's style hint
typography.monospace | the body's size | the body's weight | family Cascadia Mono | style hint Monospace
